, taking integration by parts with respect to
t
, we have

\displaystyle J_{2,1}=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\pm}\left(\pm\varepsilon\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w_{l}^{2}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right).

The second term on the right-hand side of the above equality is dominated by

\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}.

For the term
J_{2,2}
, it follows from (
2.3
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{2,2}=\;&\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)\\
\geq\;&\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}-\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(B_{C})}.\end{split}

For the term
J_{2,3}
, we can deduce from (
2.11
), (
2.12
) and (
2.25
) that

\displaystyle J_{2,3}\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{1}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

As for
J_{2,4}
and
J_{2,5}
, it follows from (
2.32
), (
2.37
), (
2.43
) and (
2.49
) that

\displaystyle J_{2,4}+J_{2,5}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\chi_{\gamma}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}.

For the terms
J_{2,6}
–
J_{2,8}
with
|\alpha|\leq N-1
, from the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
, the local conservation laws (
3.8
) and the first equation of (
3.10
), we can bound these terms by

\displaystyle C\left\{\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}(a_{\pm}^{\varepsilon},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2},

where we have used (
3.28
) and
\left|e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}\right|\approx 1
. Finally, for the term
J_{2,9}
, it follows from (
2.21
) and (
2.28
) that
J_{2,9}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)
.

Collecting all the estimates of
J_{2,1}
–
J_{2,9}
and taking summation over
|\alpha|\leq N-1
, (
3.3
) follows. This completes the proof of Lemma
3.3
.
∎

Next, we provide the weighted energy estimate with the pure spatial derivative
|\alpha|=N
.

Lemma 3.4
.

There holds

\displaystyle\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\left\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\chi_{\gamma}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\right\}\varepsilon\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}\right\|^{2}

(3.38)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(a^{\varepsilon}_{\pm},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

Proof.

Applying
\partial^{\alpha}
with
|\alpha|=N
to (
3.32
), taking the inner product with
\varepsilon e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}
over
\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}
and summing with
\pm
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\pm}\left(\partial_{t}\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm},\varepsilon e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}\right)+\sum_{\pm}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L_{\pm}\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon},\varepsilon w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\pm}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L_{\pm}\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon},\varepsilon\left(e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}-1\right)w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{\pm}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L_{\pm}\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon},\varepsilon e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}_{\pm}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

(3.39)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\pm}\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|\leq|\alpha|}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha_{1}}\left(\pm\partial^{\alpha_{1}+e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{i}}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm},\varepsilon e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\pm}\sum_{1\leq\left|\alpha_{1}\right|\leq|\alpha|}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha_{1}}\left(\mp\partial^{\alpha_{1}+e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}v_{i}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm},\varepsilon e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\pm}\left(\mp\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}v_{i}\mu^{1/2},\varepsilon e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}\right)+\sum_{\pm}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{\pm}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\varepsilon e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}\right),

where we used (
1.8
) and the identity (
3.37
).

As before, we denote these terms in (
3.3
) by
J_{3,1}
to
J_{3,8}
and estimate them term by term. For the term
J_{3,1}
, from integration by parts with respect to
t
, one has

\displaystyle J_{3,1}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\pm}^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\pm}\left(\pm\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\pm}^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\pm}^{\varepsilon}\right).

The second term on the right-hand side of the above equality is bounded by

\varepsilon^{2}\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\pm}^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}.

For the term
J_{3,2}
, it follows from (
2.3
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{3,2}=\;&\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}L\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)\\
\geq\;&\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}-\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(B_{C})}.\end{split}

For the term
J_{3,3}
, applying (
2.11
), (
2.12
) and (
2.25
), we can deduce

\displaystyle J_{3,3}\lesssim\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{1}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

For the term
J_{3,4}
, one can derive from (
2.10
) that

\displaystyle J_{3,4}\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(a_{\pm}^{\varepsilon},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(a_{\pm}^{\varepsilon},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}.

As for
J_{3,5}
and
J_{3,6}
, by using (
2.33
), (
2.38
), (
2.44
) and (
2.50
), one has

\displaystyle J_{3,5}+J_{3,6}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\chi_{\gamma}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\varepsilon\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}\right\|^{2}.

For the term
J_{3,7}
, it is straightforward to see that

\displaystyle J_{3,7}\lesssim\eta\left(\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(a_{\pm}^{\varepsilon},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}\right)+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2},

where we have used (
1.8
) and
\left|e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}\right|\approx 1
. Finally, for the term
J_{3,8}
, it follows from (
2.22
) and (
2.29
) that

J_{3,8}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)
.

Collecting all the estimates of
J_{3,1}
–
J_{3,8}
with
|\alpha|=N
, (
3.4
) follows. This completes the proof of Lemma
3.4
.
∎

Finally, we present the weighted energy estimate with the mixed derivatives
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
and
|\beta|\geq 1
.

Lemma 3.5
.

There holds

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{N}C_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N}\\
{|\beta|=m}\end{subarray}}\left\{\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\left\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\chi_{\gamma}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\right\}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N}\\
{|\beta|\geq 1}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-1}\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\!\!\|w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(a^{\varepsilon}_{\pm},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}+\left\{\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)\right\}\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

(3.40)

Here,
C_{m}
is a fixed constant satisfying
C_{m}\gg C_{m+1}
.

Proof.

Let
1\leq m\leq N
. By applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
with
|\beta|=m
and
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
to (
3.35
), taking the inner product with
e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}
over
\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}
and summing with
\pm
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{\pm}\left(\partial_{t}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\pm}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}L_{\pm}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon},w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle-\sum_{\pm}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}L_{\pm}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon},\left(e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}-1\right)w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{\pm}C_{\beta}^{e_{i}}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\pm}\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|\leq|\alpha|}C^{\alpha_{1}}_{\alpha}\left(\pm\partial^{\alpha_{1}+e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta+e_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\pm}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{|\alpha_{1}|+|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}\\
{|\beta_{1}|\leq 1}\end{subarray}}C^{\alpha_{1},\beta_{1}}_{\alpha,\beta}\left(\mp\partial_{\beta_{1}}v_{i}\partial^{\alpha_{1}+e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\pm}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{|\alpha_{1}|\leq|\alpha|}\\
{|\beta_{1}|\leq 1}\end{subarray}}C^{\alpha_{1},\beta_{1}}_{\alpha,\beta}\left(\mp\partial_{\beta_{1}}v_{i}\partial^{\alpha_{1}+e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\mathbf{P}_{\pm}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{\pm}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v_{i}\partial^{e_{i}}\mp\partial^{e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial_{e_{i}}\right]\mathbf{P}_{\pm}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\pm}\left(\mp\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial_{\beta}(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}),e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\pm}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma_{\pm}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right).

(3.41)

Here, we have used the identity

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v_{i}\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\pm\frac{1}{2}\partial^{e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}v_{i}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)=0.

Now we denote all terms in (
3.3
) by
J_{4,1}
to
J_{4,10}
and estimate them term by term. First of all, for the term
J_{4,1}
, employing integration by parts with respect to
t
, we obtain

\displaystyle J_{4,1}=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\pm}\left(\pm\varepsilon\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}w^{2}_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right).

The second term on the right-hand side of the above equality is dominated by

\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}.

For the term
J_{4,2}
, it follows from (
2.4
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{4,2}\geq\;&\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}-\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|<|\beta|}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta^{\prime})\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta^{\prime}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\\
&-\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(B_{C})}.\end{split}

For the term
J_{4,3}
, with the help of (
2.11
), (
2.12
) and (
2.25
), one obtains

\displaystyle J_{4,3}\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{1}}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta^{\prime})\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta^{\prime}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

For the term
J_{4,4}
, it can be inferred from (
1.12
) and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
that

\displaystyle J_{4,4}\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|w_{l}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{|\alpha^{\prime}|=|\alpha|+1}\\
{|\beta^{\prime}|=|\beta|-1}\end{subarray}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha^{\prime},\beta^{\prime})\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}},

where we used the fact
w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\leq w_{l}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma+2s}
for
\gamma>-3
.
For the terms
J_{4,5}
and
J_{4,6}
, making use of (
2.34
), (
2.39
), (
2.45
) and (
2.51
), we get

\displaystyle J_{4,5}+J_{4,6}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)+\chi_{\gamma}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}.

For the terms
J_{4,7}
–
J_{4,9}
with
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
,
|\beta|\geq 1
and
|\alpha|\leq N-1
, applying (
3.8
),
(
3.28
)
, the first equation of (
3.10
), the Sobolev inequalities (
2.26
) and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
, they can be bounded by

\displaystyle C\left\{\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}(a_{\pm}^{\varepsilon},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}.

Finally, for the term
J_{4,10}
, it follows from (
2.23
) and (
2.30
) that

J_{4,10}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)
.

As a consequence, by plugging all the estimates above into (
3.3
), taking the summation over
\left\{|\beta|=m,|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N\right\}
for each given
1\leq m\leq N
, and then taking combination of those
N
estimates with properly chosen constant
C_{m}>0

(1\leq m\leq N)
and
\eta
small enough,
we obtain (
3.5
). This completes the proof of Lemma
3.5
.
∎

3.4.
Energy Estimate in Negative Sobolev Space

This subsection aims to provide an energy estimate in negative Sobolev space, for both hard and soft potentials.

Lemma 3.6
.

There holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left\{\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}+\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\left\{\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)\left\{\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.42)

Proof.

Applying
\Lambda^{-\varrho}
to the first equation of (
1.3
) and taking the inner product with
\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}
over
\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Lambda^{-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1},\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right)+\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon},\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

(3.43)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left(q_{0}\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right),\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right)-\left(\frac{q_{0}}{2}\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot vf^{\varepsilon}\right),\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right)+\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Lambda^{-\varrho}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right).

Now we further estimate (
3.43
) term by term. For the left-hand second term, it follows from the first equation of (
3.10
) and (
3.12
) that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Lambda^{-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1},\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right)=\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}.

For the left-hand third term, one has from (
2.2
) that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon},\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right)\geq\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}.

To estimate the right-hand first term, owing to
1<\varrho<\frac{3}{2}
, by using (
2.15
), (
2.16
), the Minkowski inequality (
2.17
), the Hölder inequality and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
, one has

\displaystyle\left(q_{0}\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right),\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left(q_{0}\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right),\Lambda^{-\varrho}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right)+\left(q_{0}\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right),\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\mu^{\delta}f^{\varepsilon})\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}})\right\|\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\mu^{\delta}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{{L^{2}_{v}}{L^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}_{x}}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|+\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}L_{x}^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

(3.44)

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\frac{12}{3+2\varrho}}}\left\|\mu^{\delta}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}{L^{\frac{12}{3+2\varrho}}_{x}}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|+\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{\varrho}}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}(\mu^{\delta}f^{\varepsilon})\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\left\{\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\right\}+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\eta\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}.

Similarly, the right-hand second term has the same upper bound as (
3.44
). Finally, for the last term on the right-hand side of (
3.43
), by the collision invariant property, we have for soft potentials that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Lambda^{-\varrho}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right)=\;

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Lambda^{-\varrho}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left(\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\right)\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}L_{x}^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\right\|_{L_{x}^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

(3.45)

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\||f^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}}|f^{\varepsilon}|_{H^{4}}\right\|_{L_{x}^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L_{x}^{\frac{3}{\varrho}}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{4}_{v}L^{2}_{x}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2},

where we have used (
2.8
), (
2.15
), (
2.16
), the Minkowski inequality (
2.17
), the Hölder inequality and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
. Similarly, for the hard potential case, we obtain the same upper bound as (
3.4
).

As a result, combining all the estimates above, (
3.42
) follows. This completes the proof of Lemma
3.6
.
∎

3.5.
Uniform Energy Estimate

In this subsection, based on Lemma
3.1
–
3.6
, we give the uniform a priori estimates
with respect to
\varepsilon\in(0,1]
, for both hard and soft potentials.

Proposition 3.7
.

There is
\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)
satisfying (
1.13
) such that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)+\lambda\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)\lesssim\left\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\chi_{\gamma}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\right\}\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t).

(3.46)

Proof.

To prove (
3.46
), we take the proper linear combination of those estimates obtained in the previous Lemmas as follows. For
\widetilde{C}_{1}>0
and
\widetilde{C}_{2}>0
large enough,
0<\kappa_{4}\ll 1
, the combination
\widetilde{C}_{2}\times\left\{\widetilde{C}_{1}\times\big[\eqref{estimate without weight}+\kappa_{4}\times\eqref{macro estimate}\big]+\eqref{weighted estimate1}+\eqref{weighted estimate2}\right\}+\eqref{weighted estimate3}
gives

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)+\lambda\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)\lesssim\left\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\!+\!\chi_{\gamma}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\right\}\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)+\left\{\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)\right\}\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t),

(3.47)

where
\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)
is given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t):=\;&\widetilde{C}_{2}\bigg\{\widetilde{C}_{1}\Big[\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\kappa_{4}\varepsilon\mathcal{E}_{int}^{N}(t)\Big]\\
&\qquad+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-1}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\\
&\qquad+\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\sum_{\pm}\varepsilon\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f_{\pm}^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\bigg\}\\
&+\sum_{m=1}^{N}C_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N}\\
{|\beta|=m}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}w_{l}(\alpha,\beta)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}.\end{split}

(3.48)

Recalling the a priori assumption (
3.2
), the desired estimate (
3.46
) follows directly from (
3.47
). This completes the proof of Proposition
3.7
.
∎

Proposition 3.8
.

There is
\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l}(t)
satisfying (
1.3
) such that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l}(t)+\lambda\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)\lesssim\left\|\nabla_{x}(a_{\pm}^{\varepsilon},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}+\left\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\chi_{\gamma}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\right\}\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l}(t).

(3.49)

Proof.

Let
1\leq|\alpha|\leq N
in (
3.2
). Repeating those computations in Lemma
3.2
, one can obtain

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left\{\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\pm}^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}+\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f_{\pm}^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\{\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)\right\}\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

(3.50)

Moreover, taking the inner product of (
3.35
) with
e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}
over
\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}
and summing with
\pm
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\pm}\left(\partial_{t}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)+\sum_{\pm}\left(\pm\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}v_{i}\mu^{1/2},\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\pm}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L_{\pm}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon},\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle-\sum_{\pm}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L_{\pm}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon},\left(e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}-1\right)\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

(3.51)

\displaystyle-\sum_{\pm}\left(\pm\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}v_{i}\mu^{1/2},\left(e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}-1\right)\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{\pm}\left(\left[\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v_{i}\partial^{e_{i}}\mp\partial^{e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial_{e_{i}}\right]\mathbf{P}_{\pm}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\pm}\left(\mp\partial^{e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}v_{i}\mathbf{P}_{\pm}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)+\sum_{\pm}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Gamma_{\pm}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right),

where we have used

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v_{i}\partial^{e_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\pm\frac{1}{2}\partial^{e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}v_{i}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)=0

and

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial_{e_{i}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)=0.

Now we denote all terms in (
3.51
) by
J_{5,1}
to
J_{5,8}
and estimate them term by term.
For the term
J_{5,1}
, we acquire from integration by parts with respect to
t
that

\displaystyle J_{5,1}=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\pm}\left(\pm\varepsilon\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon},e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right).

The second term on the right-hand side of the above equality is bounded by

\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}.

For the term
J_{5,2}
, recalling the first equation of (
3.10
) and (
3.12
), one has

\displaystyle J_{5,2}=\sum_{\pm}\left(\pm\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}v_{i}\mu^{1/2},\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)=\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}.

For the term
J_{5,3}
, it follows from (
2.2
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{5,3}=\;&\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)\geq\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}.\end{split}

For the term
J_{5,4}
, combining (
2.11
), (
2.12
) and (
2.25
), we have

\displaystyle J_{5,4}\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{1}}\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

For the term
J_{5,5}
, we can obtain from (
2.25
) that

\displaystyle J_{5,5}=\sum_{\pm}\left(\mp\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{e_{i}}\phi^{\varepsilon}v_{i}\mu^{1/2},\left(e^{\pm\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}-1\right)\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

For the terms
J_{5,6}
and
J_{5,7}
, by utilizing (
3.8
),
(
3.28
)
, the first equation of (
3.10
), the Sobolev inequalities (
2.26
) and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
, they can be dominated by

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\left\|\nabla_{x}(a_{\pm}^{\varepsilon},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t),

where we used the definitions of
\mathbf{P}
and
\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}
.
Finally, for the term
J_{5,8}
, it follows from (
2.20
) and (
2.27
) that
J_{5,8}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)
. Therefore, combining all the estimates above, one has

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left\{\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}+\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

(3.52)

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{\pm}\left\|e^{\pm\frac{\varepsilon\phi^{\varepsilon}}{2}}\{\mathbf{I}_{\pm}-\mathbf{P}_{\pm}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}(a_{\pm}^{\varepsilon},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}+\left\{\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)\right\}\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t).

As in Proposition
3.7
, under the a priori assumption (
3.2
), a suitable linear combination of (
3.5
), (
3.52
), (
3.4
), (
3.3
), (
3.4
) and (
3.5
) yields the desired estimate (
3.49
). This completes the proof of Proposition
3.8
.
∎

Proposition 3.9
.

There is
\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l}(t)
satisfying (
1.18
) such that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l}(t)+\lambda\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{N,l}(t)\lesssim\left\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\chi_{\gamma}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\right\}\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t).

(3.53)

Proof.

Similar to Lemma
3.1
, there exists an interactive functional
\mathcal{E}^{-\varrho}_{int}(t)
satisfying

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{E}^{-\varrho}_{int}(t)\right|\lesssim\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{2}},

such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}^{-\varrho}_{int}(t)+\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}(a_{\pm}^{\varepsilon},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\varepsilon^{2}\left\|\langle\Lambda^{-\varrho}(g^{\varepsilon}_{+}+g^{\varepsilon}_{-}+h^{\varepsilon}_{+}+h^{\varepsilon}_{-}),\zeta\rangle\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.54)

By the interpolation inequality with respect to the spatial derivatives, it is easy to derive that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\lesssim\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}},\\
&\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\lesssim\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{2}_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2},\\
&\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\lesssim\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}},\\
&\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{2}-\varrho}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\lesssim\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{1}_{x}N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2},\end{split}

(3.55)

where we have used
\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}>1-\varrho
for
1<\varrho<\frac{3}{2}
.
Recalling (
3.7
) for
g^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}
and
h^{\varepsilon}_{\pm}
, we obtain from (
2.10
) and (
3.55
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\varepsilon\left\|\langle\Lambda^{-\varrho}(g^{\varepsilon}_{+}+g^{\varepsilon}_{-}),\zeta\rangle\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\;&\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\mu^{\delta}f^{\varepsilon})\right\|+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\langle\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\zeta\rangle\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\lesssim\;&\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\mu^{\delta}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}L_{x}^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}}+\left\|\langle\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\zeta\rangle\right\|_{L_{x}^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}}\\
\lesssim\;&\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\frac{12}{3+2\varrho}}}\left\|\mu^{\delta}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}L_{x}^{\frac{12}{3+2\varrho}}}+\left\||f^{\varepsilon}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{-m}}\right\|_{L_{x}^{\frac{6}{3+2\varrho}}}\\
\lesssim\;&\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}(\mu^{\delta}f^{\varepsilon})\right\|+\left\||f^{\varepsilon}|_{L^{2}_{-m}}\right\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{\frac{12}{3+2\varrho}}}\\
\lesssim\;&\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}(\mu^{\delta}f^{\varepsilon})\right\|+\left\|\Lambda^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\varrho}{2}}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{-m}}\\
\lesssim\;&\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t),\end{split}

(3.56)

and

\displaystyle\varepsilon\left\|\langle\Lambda^{-\varrho}(h^{\varepsilon}_{+}+h^{\varepsilon}_{-}),\zeta\rangle\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}.

(3.57)

Here, we used (
2.15
), (
2.16
), the Minkowski inequality (
2.17
) and the Hölder inequality. Hence, it follows from (
3.54
)–(
3.57
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}^{-\varrho}_{int}(t)+\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}(a_{\pm}^{\varepsilon},b^{\varepsilon},c^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l}(t)\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{N,l}(t).\end{split}

(3.58)

Combining (
3.55
) with the a priori assumption (
3.2
), one can deduce (
3.53
) by a proper linear combination of (
3.42
), (
3.46
) and (
3.58
). This completes the proof of Proposition
3.9
.
∎

4.
Global Existence

In this section, we give the proof of Theorem
1.1
and
1.2
by first establishing the closed uniform estimates on
X(t)
defined in (
3.1
).

Noticing the estimate (
3.49
) and the difference between
\mathcal{E}_{N,l}(t)
and
\mathcal{D}_{N,l}(t)
, we need to obtain the temporal decay estimates of
\left\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|
and
\left\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|
.

4.1.
Time Decay Estimate

Our main idea to deduce the time decay estimate is based on the approach proposed by
[
39
]
.
First of all, we give a more refined energy estimate for the pure spatial derivatives than (
3.31
).

Lemma 4.1
.

There hold

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\bigg\{\sum_{k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\bigg\}+\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\bigg\{\sum_{k\leq N+1}\left\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{1\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\bigg\}

(4.1)

and

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\bigg\{\sum_{1\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{1\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\bigg\}+\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{1\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\bigg\{\sum_{1\leq k\leq N+1}\left\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{2\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\bigg\}.

(4.2)

Proof.

Applying
\nabla^{k}
to the first equation of (
1.3
) and taking the inner product with
\nabla^{k}f^{\varepsilon}
over
\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left\{\left\|\nabla^{k}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}+\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left(q_{0}\nabla^{k}\left(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right),\nabla^{k}f^{\varepsilon}\right)-\left(\frac{q_{0}}{2}\nabla^{k}\left(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot vf^{\varepsilon}\right),\nabla^{k}f^{\varepsilon}\right)+\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla^{k}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle:=\;

\displaystyle J_{6,1}+J_{6,2}+J_{6,3}.

(4.3)

Here, we have used the collision invariant property.

Firstly, we estimate
J_{6,1}
–
J_{6,3}
with
2\leq k\leq N
for soft potentials.
For the term
J_{6,1}
, by employing the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev inequalities (
2.26
) and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, one has

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{6,1}\lesssim\;&\sum_{1\leq j\leq k}\left\|\nabla^{j}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}}\left\|\nabla^{k-j}(\mu^{\delta}f^{\varepsilon})\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\\
&+\sum_{1\leq j\leq k-1}\left\|\nabla^{j}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\nabla^{k-j}\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\\
&+\sum_{j=k}\left\|\nabla^{j}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\\
\lesssim\;&\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\bigg\{\sum_{1\leq\ell\leq N+1}\left\|\nabla^{\ell}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{k}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}\bigg\}.\end{split}

(4.4)

Similarly,
J_{6,2}
has the same upper bound as (
4.4
).

For the term
J_{6,3}
, we denote
J^{\prime}_{6,3}
,
J^{\prime\prime}_{6,3}
,
J^{\prime\prime\prime}_{6,3}
,
J^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{6,3}
to be the terms corresponding to the decomposition (
2.19
). For the term
J^{\prime}_{6,3}
, making use of the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev inequalities (
2.26
) and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we obtain the following result

\displaystyle J^{\prime}_{6,3}=\;

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla^{k}\Gamma(\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}),\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{1\leq j\leq k-1}\left\|\nabla^{j}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k-j}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\bigg\{\sum_{2\leq\ell\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{\ell}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}\bigg\}.

In a similar fashion, for the term
J^{\prime\prime}_{6,3}
, selecting the first term within the minimum function in (
(1)
) yields

\displaystyle J^{\prime\prime}_{6,3}\lesssim\;\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\bigg\{\sum_{1\leq\ell\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{\ell}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}\bigg\}.

Considering the term
J^{\prime\prime\prime}_{6,3}
and opting for the second term inside the minimum function in (
(1)
), we arrive at the following outcome

\displaystyle\begin{split}J^{\prime\prime\prime}_{6,3}\lesssim\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{1\leq j\leq k-1}\left\|\nabla^{j}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k-j}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}.\end{split}

(4.5)

The final term on the right-hand side of the above inequality can be bounded by

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.6)

Consequently, thanks to
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
, by combining (
4.5
) and (
4.6
), we can derive

\displaystyle J^{\prime\prime\prime}_{6,3}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\bigg\{\sum_{2\leq\ell\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{\ell}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}\bigg\}.

Regarding the term
J^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{6,3}
, it is crucial to estimate it with great care.

Case 1.

j=0
.  By considering the first term inside the minimum function in
(
(1)
)
, we have

\displaystyle J^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{6,3}\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 2}\left\|\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

(4.7)

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}.

Case 2.

1\leq j\leq k-3
.  By selecting the first term from within the minimum function in
(
(1)
)
, we obtain

\displaystyle J^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{6,3}\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla^{j}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k-j}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 2}\left\|\nabla^{j}\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k-j}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}N^{s}_{\gamma}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\bigg\{\sum_{3\leq\ell\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{\ell}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\bigg\}.

Case 3.

j=k-2
.   Singling out the first term inside the minimum function in
(
(1)
)
, we arrive at

\displaystyle J^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{6,3}\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla^{j}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k-j}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 2}\left\|\nabla^{j}\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\nabla^{k-j}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}N^{s}_{\gamma}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\bigg\{\sum_{2\leq\ell\leq 4}\left\|\nabla^{\ell}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\bigg\}.

Case 4.

j=k-1
.  By choosing the second term inside the minimum function in
(
(1)
)
and setting
m=-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}
, we get

\displaystyle J^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{6,3}\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla^{j}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\nabla^{k-j}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}N^{s}_{\gamma}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\nabla^{j}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 2}\left\|\nabla^{k-j}\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}N^{s}_{\gamma}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\bigg\{\left\|\nabla^{2}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\bigg\},

where we have used

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 2}\left\|\nabla^{k-j}\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}N^{s}_{\gamma}}\lesssim\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{2}_{x}H^{3}_{v}}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t).

Case 5.

j=k
.   By selecting the second term within the minimum function in
(
(1)
)
and setting
m=-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}
, we can deduce

\displaystyle J^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{6,3}\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}N^{s}_{\gamma}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 2}\left\|\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}N^{s}_{\gamma}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\bigg\{\sum_{1\leq\ell\leq 2}\left\|\nabla^{\ell}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\bigg\}.

Therefore, combining all the estimates mentioned above with the a priori assumption (
3.2
), we can conclude

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\bigg\{\sum_{2\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{2\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\bigg\}+\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{2\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\bigg\{\sum_{1\leq k\leq N+1}\left\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{2\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\bigg\}.

(4.8)

Now, let’s estimate (
4.1
) for
k=0
. Under the a priori assumption (
3.2
), by combining (
2.5
) and (
(1)
) along with the Sobolev inequalities (
2.26
), it is easy to derive that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left\{\left\|f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}+\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\left\{\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}.

(4.9)

Similarly, we can deduce that for
k=1
, there holds

\displaystyle\!\!\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left\{\left\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{2}_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}+\frac{\lambda}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\nabla_{x}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{1/2}_{N,l}(t)\left\{\left\|\nabla^{2}_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{2}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}.

(4.10)

Therefore, for the soft potential case, the desired estimates (
4.1
) and (
4.2
) follow directly by (
4.8
), (
4.9
) and (
4.10
).

Then, we turn to the hard potential case. In fact, for the hard potential case, the estimates of
J_{6,1}
–
J_{6,3}
in (
4.1
) with
2\leq k\leq N
can be derived in a similar but simpler way than the soft potential case. Moreover,
l=l_{2}\geq N+\max\{1,\gamma+2s\}
will be used in the process of estimating
J_{6,2}
and
J_{6,3}
. The details are omitted for simplicity. This completes the proof of Lemma
4.1
.
∎

Lemma 4.2
.

There exist the energy functional
\mathcal{E}^{k}_{N}(t)
and the corresponding energy dissipation rate functional
\mathcal{D}^{k}_{N}(t)
satisfying (
1.18
) and (
1.19
) respectively such that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{N}(t)+\lambda\mathcal{D}^{k}_{N}(t)\leq 0

(4.11)

holds for
k=0,1
and all
0\leq t\leq T
.

Furthermore, we can obtain that for
k=0,1
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{k}_{N}(t)\lesssim(1+t)^{-(k+\varrho)}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l}(\tau).

(4.12)

Here
l=l_{2}
for hard potentials
\gamma+2s\geq 0
, while
l=l_{0}+l_{1}
for soft potentials
-3<\gamma<-2s
.

Proof.

Similar to Lemma
3.1
, by recalling (
3.27
) for
\mathcal{E}^{N}_{int}(t)
and considering the a priori assumption (
3.2
), we obtain a more precise estimate compared to (
3.3
),

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}^{N}_{int}(t)+\sum_{1\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{k\leq N+1}\left\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.13)

Moreover, similar to Lemma
3.1
, there is an interactive energy functional
\mathcal{E}^{1\rightarrow N}_{int}(t)
satisfying

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{E}^{1\rightarrow N}_{int}(t)\right|\lesssim\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N+1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}

such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}^{1\rightarrow N}_{int}(t)+\sum_{2\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{1\leq k\leq N+1}\left\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{1\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{1\leq k\leq N}\left\|\nabla^{k}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{N^{s}_{\gamma}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.14)

Therefore, recalling (
1.16
) for
\mathcal{E}^{k}_{N}(t)
, (
4.11
) for
k=0
can be derived from (
4.1
), (
4.13
) and the a priori assumption (
3.2
).
Similarly, (
4.11
) for
k=1
follows from (
4.2
), (
4.14
) and the a priori assumption (
3.2
).

To deduce (
4.12
), for
k=0,1
, one has

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{k}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\lesssim\left\|\nabla^{k+1}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+1+\varrho}}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{\frac{1}{k+1+\varrho}}.

The above inequality together with the fact that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{m}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\nabla^{m}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\right)^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+1+\varrho}}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}(k+\varrho)}\nabla^{m}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}\}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|\right)^{\frac{1}{k+1+\varrho}}

implies

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{k}_{N}(t)\lesssim\left\{\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l}(t)\right\}^{\frac{1}{k+1+\varrho}}\left\{\mathcal{D}^{k}_{N}(t)\right\}^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+1+\varrho}}\lesssim\left\{\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l}(\tau)\right\}^{\frac{1}{k+1+\varrho}}\left\{\mathcal{D}^{k}_{N}(t)\right\}^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+1+\varrho}}.

Hence, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{N}(t)+\lambda\left\{\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l}(\tau)\right\}^{-\frac{1}{k+\varrho}}\left\{\mathcal{E}^{k}_{N}(t)\right\}^{1+\frac{1}{k+\varrho}}\leq 0.

(4.15)

Therefore, (
4.12
) can be deduced by solving the above inequality directly. This completes the proof of Lemma
4.2
.
∎

4.2.
Proof of Global Existence for Soft Potentials

In this subsection, we prove Theorem
1.1
by closing the a priori estimates on
X(t)
in the following lemma. The idea of the following proof is similar to the Boltzmann case in
[
60
]
.

Lemma 4.3
.

Let
0<T\leq\infty
. Assume that there exists a suitable small constant
\delta_{0}>0
such that the a priori assumption (
3.2
) holds. Then one has

\displaystyle X(t)\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0)+X^{\frac{3}{2}}(t),

(4.16)

for any
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

We divide it into three steps.

Step 1.
It follows from the first equation of (
3.10
) and (
3.12
) that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}=-\Delta_{x}^{-1}\partial_{t}(a_{+}^{\varepsilon}-a_{-}^{\varepsilon})=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Delta^{-1}_{x}\nabla_{x}\cdot G^{\varepsilon},

(4.17)

where
G^{\varepsilon}
is given by (
3.11
). Then by the Sobolev inequality and the Riesz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\varepsilon^{2}\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}&\lesssim\left\|G^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\nabla_{x}G^{\varepsilon}\right\|\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l}(t)\lesssim X(t)(1+t)^{-(\varrho+p)},\\
\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}&\lesssim\left\|\nabla^{2}_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\nabla^{3}_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l}(t)\lesssim X(t)(1+t)^{-(\varrho+p)}.\end{split}

(4.18)

Here
1<\varrho<3/2
,
1/2<p<1
and
\varrho+p>2
.
By applying Proposition
3.9
in the case when
l=l_{0}+l_{1}
, one has from (
3.53
) that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(t)+\lambda\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(t)\lesssim\left\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\right\}\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(t).

This, together with (
4.18
) and the Gronwall inequality, as well as (
3.2
), implies that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0)e^{C\int_{0}^{t}\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\}{\mathrm{d}}\tau}\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0)

(4.19)

for any
0\leq t\leq T
.

Step 2.
We begin with (
3.49
) with
l=l_{0}
in Proposition
3.8
, i.e.

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)+\lambda\mathcal{D}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\lesssim\left\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\right\}\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t).

(4.20)

As in
[
60
,
61
]
, at time
t
, we split the velocity space
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
into

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(t)=\left\{\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma+2s)}\leq t^{1-p}\right\},\quad\mathbb{E}^{c}(t)=\left\{\langle v\rangle^{-(\gamma+2s)}>t^{1-p}\right\},

(4.21)

where
-3<\gamma<-2s
and
1/2<p<1
. Corresponding to this splitting, we define
\mathcal{E}^{h,low}_{N,l_{0}}(t)
to be the restriction of
\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)
to
\mathbb{E}(t)
and similarly
\mathcal{E}^{h,high}_{N,l_{0}}(t)
to be the restriction of
\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)
to
\mathbb{E}^{c}(t)
.
Then, thanks to (
4.21
) and (
1.3
), it follows from (
4.20
) that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)+\lambda pt^{p-1}\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\lesssim\left\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\right\}\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)+t^{p-1}\mathcal{E}^{h,high}_{N,l_{0}}(t),

which by solving the ODE inequality, gives

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\!\int_{0}^{t}\!e^{-\lambda(t^{p}-\tau^{p})}\!\left(\!\left\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\!+\left\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\!+\!\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\!\right\}\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(\tau)+\!\tau^{p-1}\mathcal{E}^{h,high}_{N,l_{0}}(\tau)\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle+e^{-\lambda t^{p}}\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(0).

(4.22)

In what follows we estimate these three terms in the time integral on the right-hand side of (
4.2
).
First of all, making use of (
4.18
), one has

\displaystyle\{\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\}\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\lesssim\{\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\}^{\frac{3}{2}}\lesssim X^{\frac{3}{2}}(t)(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}(\varrho+p)}.

(4.23)

Next, one can prove

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{h,high}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0)(1+t)^{-(\varrho+p)}.

(4.24)

In fact, it is straightforward to see from (
4.19
) that for any
0\leq t\leq T
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{h,high}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\lesssim\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(t)\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(t)\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0).

On the other hand, noticing that
t^{\varrho+p}\leq\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\varrho+p}{1-p}(\gamma+2s)}
on
\mathbb{E}^{c}(t)
and
l_{1}
is defined in (
1.20
),
one has from (
4.19
) that

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{h,high}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0)t^{-(\varrho+p)}.

Therefore, (
4.24
) holds true. Finally, by employing (
4.12
) and (
4.19
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\lesssim(1+t)^{-(1+\varrho)}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(\tau)\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0)(1+t)^{-(1+\varrho)}.

(4.25)

Notice that one has the following inequalities:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda(t^{p}-\tau^{p})}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{3}{2}(\varrho+p)}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\lesssim(1+t)^{-(\frac{3\varrho}{2}-1+\frac{5p}{2})},\\
&\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda(t^{p}-\tau^{p})}\tau^{p-1}(1+\tau)^{-(\varrho+p)}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\lesssim(1+t)^{-(\varrho+p)},\\
&\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda(t^{p}-\tau^{p})}(1+\tau)^{-(1+\varrho)}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\lesssim(1+t)^{-(\varrho+p)}.\end{split}

(4.26)

Then, plugging (
4.23
), (
4.24
) and (
4.25
) into (
4.2
) and using (
4.26
), one has

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\lesssim\left\{\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0)+X^{\frac{3}{2}}(t)\right\}(1+t)^{-(\varrho+p)}

(4.27)

for any
0\leq t\leq T
. Here we have used
1<\varrho<3/2
and
1/2<p<1
.

Step 3.
By employing (
4.12
) and (
4.19
), one can obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\lesssim(1+t)^{-\varrho}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(\tau)\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0)(1+t)^{-\varrho}.

(4.28)

Noticing
\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\approx\left\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}^{h}_{N,l_{0}}(t)
, (
4.28
) together with (
4.27
) give

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{0}}(t)\lesssim\left\{\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0)+X^{\frac{3}{2}}(t)\right\}(1+t)^{-\varrho}.

(4.29)

Now, recalling (
3.1
), the desired estimate (
4.16
) follows from (
4.19
), (
4.27
) and (
4.29
). This completes the proof of Lemma
4.3
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.1
.

It follows immediately from the a priori estimate (
4.3
) that

X(t)\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0)

holds for any
0\leq t\leq T
, as long as
\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(0)
is sufficiently small. The rest is to prove the local existence and uniqueness of solutions in terms of the energy norm
\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{0}+l_{1}}(t)
and the non-negativity of
F^{\varepsilon}=\mu+\varepsilon\mu^{1/2}f^{\varepsilon}
. One can use the iteration method with the iterating sequence
f_{n}^{\varepsilon}

(n\geq 0)
on the system

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\displaystyle\partial_{t}f_{n+1}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}{f}_{n+1}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla_{x}\phi_{n+1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L{f}_{n+1}^{\varepsilon}\\[8.53581pt]
\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad=q_{0}\nabla_{x}\phi_{n}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}{f}_{n+1}^{\varepsilon}-\frac{1}{2}q_{0}\nabla_{x}\phi_{n}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v{f}_{n+1}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Gamma\left({f}_{n}^{\varepsilon},{f}_{n}^{\varepsilon}\right),\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle-\Delta_{x}\phi_{n+1}^{\varepsilon}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f_{n+1}^{\varepsilon}\cdot q_{1}\mu^{1/2}{\mathrm{d}}v,\quad\lim_{|x|\rightarrow\infty}\phi_{n+1}^{\varepsilon}=0,\\[8.53581pt]
\displaystyle f_{n+1}^{\varepsilon}(0,x,v)=f^{\varepsilon}_{0}(x,v).\end{array}\right.

The details of proof are omitted for brevity; see
[
31
,
37
]
and
[
44
]
. Therefore, the global existence of solutions follows with the help of the continuity argument, and the estimates (
1.21
), (
1.22
) and (
1.23
) hold by the definition of
X(t)
in (
3.1
). This completes the proof of Theorem
1.1
.
∎

4.3.
Proof of Global Existence for Hard Potentials

In this subsection, we prove Theorem
1.2
by closing the a priori estimates on
X(t)
in the following lemma. We provide a simpler proof than that of Lemma
4.3
, but it is only applicable to the hard potential case.

Lemma 4.4
.

Let
0<T\leq\infty
. Assume that there exists a suitable small constant
\delta_{0}>0
such that the a priori assumption (
3.2
) holds. Then we have

\displaystyle X(t)\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{2}}(0),

(4.34)

for any
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

Step 1.
Similar to (
4.18
), due to
l=l_{2}
for hard potentials, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\varepsilon^{2}\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\left\|G^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\nabla_{x}G^{\varepsilon}\right\|\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{N,l_{2}}(t).\end{split}

(4.35)

By applying Proposition
3.9
, thanks to
l=l_{2}
, one has from (
3.53
), (
4.35
) and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{2}}(t)+\lambda\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\lesssim\varepsilon\left\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\lesssim\mathcal{D}^{1/2}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\lesssim\frac{\lambda}{2}\mathcal{D}_{N,l_{2}}(t)+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\right\}^{2},

which, together with (
3.1
) and the Gronwall inequality, as well as (
3.2
), implies that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{2}}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{N,l_{2}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{2}}(0)e^{C\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau}\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{2}}(0)

(4.36)

for any
0\leq t\leq T
.

Step 2.
It follows from (
3.46
) with
l=l_{2}
in Proposition
3.7
, (
4.35
) and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(t)+\lambda\mathcal{D}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\lesssim\left\{\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\right\}^{2}.

(4.37)

From (
1.13
) and (
1.3
) for hard potentials, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\lesssim\left\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{D}_{N,l_{2}}(t).

(4.38)

Combining (
4.37
) with (
4.38
), one has

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(t)+\lambda\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\lesssim\left\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\right\}^{2}.

By using the a priori assumption (
3.2
) and solving the ODE inequality, we can deduce

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\lesssim e^{-\lambda t}\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda(t-\tau)}\left\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau.

(4.39)

Making use of (
4.12
), (
4.36
) and (
4.39
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\lesssim\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{2}}(0)(1+t)^{-\varrho},

(4.40)

where we used the following inequality

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda(t-\tau)}(1+\tau)^{-\varrho}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\lesssim(1+t)^{-\varrho}.

Now, recalling (
3.1
), the desired estimate (
4.34
) follows from (
4.36
) and (
4.40
). This completes the proof of Lemma
4.4
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.2
.

The proof of Theorem
1.2
is similar to the proof of Theorem
1.1
. We omit the details for brevity.
∎

5.
Limit to Two-fluid Incompressible NSFP System with Ohm’s Law

In this section, our goal is to derive the two-fluid incompressible NSFP system with Ohm’s law from the VPB system (
1.3
) as
\varepsilon\to 0
. For brevity, we only consider soft potentials. The proof about hard potentials is similar and we omit the details. The approach is similar to that one in
[
44
]
, but here we provide a full proof for completeness.

Firstly, we introduce the linearized Boltzmann collision operators
\mathcal{L}
and
\mathscr{L}
by

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}h:=\;

\displaystyle-\mu^{-1/2}\left\{Q\left(\mu,\mu^{1/2}h\right)+Q\left(\mu^{1/2}h,\mu\right)\right\},

(5.1)

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}h:=\;

\displaystyle-2\mu^{-1/2}Q\left(\mu,\mu^{1/2}h\right)

(5.2)

for a scalar function
h
. The linearized Boltzmann collision operators
\mathcal{L}
and
\mathscr{L}
are also non-negative and self-adjoint. Furthermore, their null spaces are given by

\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{L})=\mathrm{span}\left\{\mu^{1/2},v_{i}\mu^{1/2}(1\leq i\leq 3),|v|^{2}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\;\;\;\;\;\;\mathcal{N}(\mathscr{L})=\mathrm{span}\left\{\mu^{1/2}\right\}.

For a given scalar function
h
, there is macro-micro decomposition

h=P_{0}h+\{I-P_{0}\}h,

(5.3)

first introduced in
[
35
]
.
Here,
P_{0}
denotes the orthogonal projection from
L^{2}(\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3})
to
\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{L})
, that is,

P_{0}h:=\left\{\widetilde{a}(t,x)+v\cdot\widetilde{b}(t,x)+\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\widetilde{c}(t,x)\right\}\mu^{1/2},

(5.4)

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{a}(t,x):=\;

\displaystyle\langle\mu^{1/2},h\rangle=\langle\mu^{1/2},P_{0}h\rangle,

\displaystyle\widetilde{b}(t,x):=\;
